# Awesome Mac Apps

Mac apps for everyday work, creative projects, and development — collected in one place by [Balaji Sivasakthi](https://github.com/balaji-sivasakthi).

[Submit your app](CONTRIBUTING.md#submit-your-app) · [Suggest an app](https://github.com/balaji-sivasakthi/awesome-mac-apps/issues/new?template=app-suggestion.yml) · [Developer guidelines](CONTRIBUTING.md) · [Run the marketplace locally](marketplace/README.md) · [Command-line apps](command-line-apps.md) · [Editor plugins](editor-plugin.md)

Browse **2,825 listings** across Mac apps, terminal tools, and editor plugins. I'm building this collection to make useful Mac software easier to find. Browse by what you want to do, explore a few options, and follow the official links to learn more. Free apps, open-source projects, and paid tools all have a place here.

Built something for Mac? [Submit your app](CONTRIBUTING.md#submit-your-app). Found a broken link or an app worth adding? Open an issue or send a pull request.

## Choose your workflow

| I want to… | Start here |
| --- | --- |
| Write, take notes, or read | [Writing & research](#reading-and-writing-tools) |
| Build and ship software | [Developer tools](#developer-tools) · [Terminal tools](command-line-apps.md) |
| Design, capture, or create | [Design & product](#design-and-product) · [Audio & video](#audio-and-video-tools) |
| Organize my Mac | [Utilities](#utilities) · [Window management](#window-management) |
| Explore AI apps | [AI tools](#ai-tools) |

Each category uses the same four columns: **App**, **Purpose**, **Availability**, and **Resources**. “Free option” reflects a listed free version or personal-use license; “See website” means pricing isn't specified here. Website links, source links, store links, and native-app labels appear in Resources. Check the official pages for current compatibility and pricing.

## Contributing

Built an app, tool, or plugin for Mac? I'd like to hear about it. Read the [submission guide](CONTRIBUTING.md), [suggest an app](https://github.com/balaji-sivasakthi/awesome-mac-apps/issues/new?template=app-suggestion.yml), or send a pull request. Keep the description short, explain what’s free or paid, and let me know if you’re involved with the project.
